Diagrammatic representation of the terms
\sim\zeta_{2}
in the left panel and the contribution

\sim\frac{s_{13}}{\ell^{2}(\ell{+}k_{1})^{2}}
to the third line in
the
\alpha^{\prime}
-expansion (
4.7
) in the right panel.

4.1.3
Higher orders

In order to compactly denote higher-order
{\alpha^{\prime}}
-corrections to
(
4.7
), we write
10
10

10

Note that the
1/2
and
1/4

multiplying the two-mass bubble and tadpole diagrams are the symmetry factors that
compensate the overcounting in the sum over cyclic permutations.

\displaystyle\widehat{A}^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{\textrm{eff}}

\displaystyle(1,2,3,4)=\int\mathrm{d}^{D}\ell\;\Bigg\{\frac{1}{\ell^{2}(\ell{+}k_{1})^{2}(\ell{+}k_{12})^{2}(\ell{+}k_{123})^{2}}+\left[\frac{N_{\text{triangle}}(1,2,34)}{\ell^{2}(\ell{+}k_{1})^{2}(\ell{+}k_{12})^{2}}\right.

(4.12)

\displaystyle\hskip-10.00002pt\left.+\frac{1}{2}\frac{N_{\text{bubble}}(12,34)}{\ell^{2}(\ell{+}k_{12})^{2}}+\frac{N_{\text{bubble}}(1,234)}{\ell^{2}(\ell{+}k_{1})^{2}}+\frac{1}{4}\frac{N_{\text{tadpole}}(1234)}{\ell^{2}}+\text{cyc}_{\ell}(1,2,3,4)\right]\Bigg\}+\mathcal{O}({\alpha^{\prime}}^{9})\,.

In an ancillary file of the arXiv submission, we provide complete results for the numerators up to
{\alpha^{\prime}}^{8}
order, which are organized for each diagram
\Gamma\in\{{\rm triangle},{\rm bubble},{\rm tadpole}\}
via

\displaystyle N_{\Gamma}

\displaystyle={\alpha^{\prime}}^{2}\zeta_{2}N_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{2})}+{\alpha^{\prime}}^{3}\zeta_{3}N_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{3})}+{\alpha^{\prime}}^{4}\zeta_{2}^{2}N_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{2}^{2})}+{\alpha^{\prime}}^{5}\Big[\zeta_{5}N_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{5})}+\zeta_{2}\zeta_{3}N_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{2}\zeta_{3})}\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+{\alpha^{\prime}}^{6}\Big[\zeta_{3}^{2}N_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{3}^{2})}+\zeta_{2}^{3}N_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{2}^{3})}\Big]+{\alpha^{\prime}}^{7}\Big[\zeta_{7}N_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{7})}+\zeta_{2}\zeta_{5}N_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{2}\zeta_{5})}+\zeta_{2}^{2}\zeta_{3}N_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{2}^{2}\zeta_{3})}\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+{\alpha^{\prime}}^{8}\Big[\zeta_{3,5}N_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{3,5})}+\zeta_{3}\zeta_{5}N_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{3}\zeta_{5})}+\zeta_{2}\zeta_{3}^{2}N_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{2}\zeta_{3}^{2})}\Big]\,,

(4.13)

with a separate numerator for each element in the tentative

\mathbb{Q}
-bases of MZVs
Blumlein:2009cf
at given weight. Note that up to
{\alpha^{\prime}}^{8}
order only the topologies given in
eq.

4.12
show up. The
expressions in
eq.

4.7
are equivalent to

\displaystyle N^{(\zeta_{2})}_{\text{triangle}}(1,2,34)

\displaystyle=-s_{12}\,,\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ N^{(\zeta_{2})}_{\text{bubble}}(12,34)=N^{(\zeta_{2})}_{\text{bubble}}(123,4)=N^{(\zeta_{2})}_{\text{tadpole}}(1234)=0\,,

\displaystyle N^{(\zeta_{3})}_{\text{triangle}}(1,2,34)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}s_{34}(s_{3,\ell}-s_{4,\ell}+s_{34})\,,\ \ \ \ \ \ N^{(\zeta_{3})}_{\text{bubble}}(12,34)=0\,,

(4.14)

\displaystyle N^{(\zeta_{3})}_{\text{bubble}}(1,234)

\displaystyle=-\frac{3}{2}s_{13}\,,\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \,N^{(\zeta_{3})}_{\text{tadpole}}(1234)=0\,,

and the first higher-order correction beyond (
4.7
) which
follows from the
{\alpha^{\prime}}
-expansion of the six-point disk integrals in
(
4.6
) is given by

\displaystyle N^{(\zeta_{2}^{2})}_{\text{triangle}}(1,2,34)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{20}s_{34}(4s_{3,\ell}^{2}+4s_{4,\ell}^{2}-7s_{3,\ell}s_{34}-s_{4,\ell}s_{34}+11s_{34}^{2})\,,

\displaystyle N^{(\zeta_{2}^{2})}_{\text{bubble}}(12,34)

\displaystyle=s_{12}^{2}\,,\ \ \ \ \ \ N^{(\zeta_{2}^{2})}_{\text{tadpole}}(1234)=\frac{24}{5}s_{13}\,,

(4.15)

\displaystyle N^{(\zeta_{2}^{2})}_{\text{bubble}}(1,234)

\displaystyle=\frac{20s_{2,\ell}s_{24}{-}4s_{3,\ell}(4s_{34}{+}s_{23}){-}13s_{34}^{2}{-}4s_{1,\ell}(2s_{34}{+}3s_{23}){-}12s_{23}s_{34}{-}9s_{23}^{2}}{20}\,.

Apart from the triangle diagram (with a
\zeta_{2}^{2}D^{4}F^{4}
vertex in the place of
\zeta_{2}F^{4}
in Figure
3a
), the order of
\zeta_{2}^{2}=\frac{5}{2}\zeta_{4}
is the first instance of a two-mass bubble diagram with a double insertion of
\zeta_{2}F^{4}
depicted in Figure
4a
. Moreover, the tadpole numerator receives contributions from the
\zeta_{2}^{2}F^{6}
vertex in Figure
4b
.

Starting from the order of

, the open-string effective action exhibits two combinations of

and

1
),

\displaystyle N^{(\zeta_{5})}_{\text{triangle}}(1,2,34)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}s_{34}\Big[s_{3,\ell}^{3}-s_{4,\ell}^{3}-(s_{3,\ell}^{2}-2s_{4,\ell}^{2})s_{34}+(s_{3,\ell}-2s_{4,\ell})s_{34}^{2}+s_{34}^{3}\Big]\,,

\displaystyle N^{(\zeta_{5})}_{\text{bubble}}(12,34)

\displaystyle=0\,,\ \ \ \ \ \ N^{(\zeta_{5})}_{\text{tadpole}}(1234)=2(s_{12}^{2}+4s_{12}s_{23}+s_{23}^{2})\,,

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4}\Big[s_{34}(8s_{2,\ell}^{2}+10s_{3,\ell}^{2}+6s_{4,\ell}^{2}+12s_{2,\ell}s_{3,\ell}+6s_{3,\ell}s_{4,\ell})

(4.16)

\displaystyle\qquad+s_{23}(6s_{2,\ell}^{2}+8s_{4,\ell}^{2}+10s_{3,\ell}^{2}+12s_{3,\ell}s_{4,\ell}+6s_{2,\ell}s_{3,\ell})

\displaystyle\qquad+s_{34}^{2}(3s_{2,\ell}-9s_{3,\ell}-s_{4,\ell})-s_{23}^{2}(5s_{2,\ell}-s_{3,\ell}-s_{4,\ell})

\displaystyle\qquad-s_{23}s_{34}(4s_{2,\ell}+14s_{3,\ell}+2s_{4,\ell})-2s_{24}(4s_{24}^{2}+3s_{34}^{2})\Big]\,,

as well as

\displaystyle N^{(\zeta_{2}\zeta_{3})}_{\text{triangle}}(1,2,34)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}s_{34}^{2}\Big[s_{3,\ell}^{2}+s_{4,\ell}^{2}-(s_{3,\ell}+s_{4,\ell})s_{34}\Big]\,,

\displaystyle N^{(\zeta_{2}\zeta_{3})}_{\text{bubble}}(12,34)

\displaystyle=-s_{12}^{2}(s_{1,\ell}+s_{3,\ell}+s_{12})\,,\ \ \ \ \ \ N^{(\zeta_{2}\zeta_{3})}_{\text{tadpole}}(1234)=0\,,

(4.17)

\displaystyle N^{(\zeta_{2}\zeta_{3})}_{\text{bubble}}(1,234)

\displaystyle=\frac{s_{34}^{2}(s_{2,\ell}{-}2s_{4,\ell}){-}s_{23}^{2}(s_{4,\ell}{-}2s_{2,\ell}){+}2s_{23}s_{34}(s_{2,\ell}{-}s_{4,\ell}){-}s_{23}(s_{23}^{2}{-}6s_{34}s_{24})}{2}\,,

and similar expressions for the numerators at order
{\alpha^{\prime}}^{6}
can be
found in appendix
A.1
. Note that the two-mass bubble
numerator
N^{(\zeta_{2}\zeta_{3})}_{\text{bubble}}(12,34)
in
(
4.17
) captures one insertion of both
\zeta_{2}F^{4}
and

\zeta_{3}D^{2}F^{4}
similar to Figure
4a
.

The simplest interaction

{\alpha^{\prime}}^{8}\zeta_{3,5}D^{10}F^{5}
with an irreducible MZV beyond depth one gives
rise to the external-bubble and tadpole numerators in
section

A.1
.
By unitarity, the absence of triangles and two-mass bubbles
\sim\zeta_{3,5}

is a consequence of only having
\zeta_{w}
of depth one in the four-point tree-level
amplitude. The contributions at
{\alpha^{\prime}}^{7}
and
the remaining terms at
{\alpha^{\prime}}^{8}

are sufficiently complicated that we do not reproduce them in text,
but instead include them only in the ancillary files.

4.2
Four points, closed strings

The double copy of the four-point half integrand (
4.2
) leads to
the following simple form of the gravitational one-loop matrix element (
3.2
),

\displaystyle M^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{4,{\rm eff}}

\displaystyle=|t_{8}(f_{1},f_{2},f_{3},f_{4})|^{2}\widehat{M}^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{4,{\rm eff}}\,,

(4.18)

\displaystyle\widehat{M}^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{4,{\rm eff}}

\displaystyle=\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{D}\ell}{\ell^{2}}\lim_{k_{\pm}\rightarrow\pm\ell}\sum_{\gamma,\rho\in S_{4}}J(+,\gamma(1,2,3,4),-|+,\rho(1,2,3,4),-)\,,

where
|t_{8}(f_{1},f_{2},f_{3},f_{4})|^{2}
instructs to replace
\epsilon_{j}^{\mu}\rightarrow\bar{\epsilon}_{j}^{\mu}
in
one of the chiral halves (with analogous replacements for the fermionic completion). By the
single-valued map in (
2.28
), the leading
{\alpha^{\prime}}
-orders of the six-point sphere integrals
in (
4.18
) can be obtained by setting
\zeta_{2}\rightarrow 0
and
\zeta_{3}\rightarrow 2\zeta_{3}

in (
4.5
). More specifically, by comparing the permutation sum over
\gamma
in (
4.18
) with the cyclic sums over
\gamma
in (
4.4
) and (
4.6
), we are led to

\displaystyle\widehat{M}^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{4,{\rm eff}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\rho\in S_{4}}{\rm sv}\,\widehat{A}^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{\rm eff}\big(1,\rho(2,3,4)\big)\,,

(4.19)

\displaystyle M^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{4,{\rm eff}}

\displaystyle=t_{8}(\bar{f}_{1},\bar{f}_{2},\bar{f}_{3},\bar{f}_{4})\sum_{\rho\in S_{4}}{\rm sv}\,A^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{\rm eff}\big(1,\rho(2,3,4)\big)\,.

These relations holds to all orders in
\alpha^{\prime}
,
and one can readily assemble the orders of
\alpha^{\prime\leq 6}
from (
4.7
), (
4.16
) and (
A.3
).
However, the permutation sum in (
4.19
) leads to additional cancellations and improved power counting
as one can for instance see from the explicit form of the first orders of the
{\alpha^{\prime}}
-expansion

\displaystyle\widehat{M}^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{4,{\rm eff}}=\int\mathrm{d}^{D}\ell\,\bigg[

\displaystyle\,\frac{1}{4}\frac{1}{\ell^{2}(\ell{+}k_{1})^{2}(\ell{+}k_{12})^{2}(\ell{+}k_{123})^{2}}+\frac{{\alpha^{\prime}}^{3}\zeta_{3}s_{12}^{2}}{\ell^{2}(\ell{+}k_{1})^{2}(\ell{+}k_{12})^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{{\alpha^{\prime}}^{5}\zeta_{5}s_{12}^{2}[s_{3,\ell}^{2}+s_{4,\ell}^{2}-(s_{3,\ell}+s_{4,\ell})s_{12}+2s_{12}^{2}]}{2\ell^{2}(\ell{+}k_{1})^{2}(\ell{+}k_{12})^{2}}

(4.20)

\displaystyle-\frac{5{\alpha^{\prime}}^{5}\zeta_{5}s_{12}s_{13}s_{14}}{2\ell^{2}(\ell{+}k_{1})^{2}}+{\cal O}({\alpha^{\prime}}^{6})\bigg]+\text{perm}(1,2,3,4)\,.

The coefficients of
\zeta_{3}
and
\zeta_{5}
are the one-loop matrix elements with a single insertion
of the operators
R^{4}
and
D^{4}R^{4}
in the low-energy effective action (
1.1
), respectively.
Similar to the power counting of the triangle numerators from
F^{4}
in (
4.7
), the eight
derivatives of
R^{4}
conspire to the external momenta in the numerator
{\alpha^{\prime}}^{3}\zeta_{3}s_{12}^{2}

of the first line of (
4.20
). As a closed-string analogue of the
D^{2}F^{4}
numerator in (
4.7
) which is
linear in loop momentum, the triangle numerator from
D^{4}R^{4}
in the second line of (
4.20
)
is quadratic in
\ell
akin to the double-copy property of supergravity numerators. The diagrams
in (
4.20
) are identical to those in Figure
3

with
R^{4}
and
D^{4}R^{4}
in the place of
F^{4}
and
D^{2}F^{4}
, respectively.

By analogy with (
4.12
) and (
4.13
) we organize higher orders in
{\alpha^{\prime}}

according to
11
11

11

The overall factor of 2 reconciles the
symmetry factors of the diagrams in the square brackets
(compensating for an overcounting by the permutation sum)
with the normalization of the one-loop matrix
element (
3.2
).

\displaystyle\widehat{M}^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{4,{\rm eff}}

\displaystyle=2\int\mathrm{d}^{D}\ell\left[\frac{1}{8}\frac{1}{\ell^{2}(\ell{+}k_{1})^{2}(\ell{+}k_{12})^{2}(\ell{+}k_{123})^{2}}+\frac{1}{4}\frac{{\cal N}_{\text{triangle}}(1,2,34)}{\ell^{2}(\ell{+}k_{1})^{2}(\ell{+}k_{12})^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\quad\left.+\frac{1}{16}\frac{{\cal N}_{\text{bubble}}(12,34)}{\ell^{2}(\ell{+}k_{12})^{2}}+\frac{1}{12}\frac{{\cal N}_{\text{bubble}}(1,234)}{\ell^{2}(\ell{+}k_{1})^{2}}+\mathcal{O}({\alpha^{\prime}}^{9})\right]+\text{perm}(1,2,3,4)\,,

(4.21)

and we provide complete results up to
{\alpha^{\prime}}^{8}
order,

\displaystyle{\cal N}_{\Gamma}

\displaystyle={\alpha^{\prime}}^{3}\zeta_{3}{\cal N}_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{3})}+{\alpha^{\prime}}^{5}\zeta_{5}{\cal N}_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{5})}+{\alpha^{\prime}}^{6}\zeta_{3}^{2}{\cal N}_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{3}^{2})}+{\alpha^{\prime}}^{7}\zeta_{7}{\cal N}_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{7})}+{\alpha^{\prime}}^{8}\zeta_{3}\zeta_{5}{\cal N}_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{3}\zeta_{5})}\,.

(4.22)

The subleading orders of (
4.20
) translate into

{\cal N}^{(\zeta_{3})}_{\text{triangle}}(1,2,34)=2s_{12}^{2}\,,\quad\quad{\cal N}^{(\zeta_{3})}_{\text{bubble}}(12,34)={\cal N}^{(\zeta_{3})}_{\text{bubble}}(1,234)=0\,,

(4.23)

as well as

\displaystyle{\cal N}^{(\zeta_{5})}_{\text{triangle}}(1,2,34)

\displaystyle=s_{12}^{2}\left[s_{3,\ell}^{2}+s_{4,\ell}^{2}-(s_{3,\ell}+s_{4,\ell})s_{12}+2s_{12}^{2}\right]\,,

\displaystyle{\cal N}^{(\zeta_{5})}_{\text{bubble}}(1,234)

\displaystyle=-15s_{12}s_{13}s_{14}\,,\ \ \ \ \ \ {\cal N}^{(\zeta_{5})}_{\text{bubble}}(12,34)=0\,.

(4.24)

At higher orders, similar calculations yield

\displaystyle{\cal N}^{(\zeta_{3}^{2})}_{\text{triangle}}(1,2,34)

\displaystyle=s_{12}^{3}\left[s_{3,\ell}^{2}+s_{4,\ell}^{2}-(s_{3,\ell}+s_{4,\ell})s_{12}\right],

\displaystyle{\cal N}^{(\zeta_{3}^{2})}_{\text{bubble}}(12,34)

\displaystyle=-4s_{12}(s_{1,\ell}+s_{2,\ell})(s_{1,\ell}+s_{2,\ell}+2s_{12})\,,

(4.25)

\displaystyle{\cal N}^{(\zeta_{3}^{2})}_{\text{bubble}}(1,234)

\displaystyle=s_{12}^{4}+s_{13}^{4}+s_{14}^{4}+3s_{1,\ell}s_{12}s_{13}s_{14}+2s_{\ell,1}\left[s_{2,\ell}s_{12}^{2}+s_{3,\ell}s_{13}^{2}+s_{4,\ell}s_{14}^{2}\right]\,,

where the two-mass bubble

{\cal N}^{(\zeta_{3}^{2})}_{\text{bubble}}(12,34)
in the second line is
due to double insertion of
\zeta_{3}R^{4}
and can be viewed as a
closed-string analogue of the double insertion of
F^{4}
in Figure
4a
. Two-mass bubbles of this
type do not occur along with single zeta values such as

\displaystyle{\cal N}^{(\zeta_{7})}_{\text{triangle}}(1,2,34)

\displaystyle=s_{12}^{2}\Big[s_{3,\ell}^{4}+s_{4,\ell}^{4}-2(s_{3,\ell}^{3}{+}s_{4,\ell}^{3})s_{12}+3(s_{3,\ell}^{2}{+}s_{4,\ell}^{2})s_{12}^{2}-2(s_{3,\ell}{+}s_{4,\ell})s_{12}^{3}+2s_{12}^{4}\Big],

\displaystyle{\cal N}^{(\zeta_{7})}_{\text{bubble}}(12,34)

\displaystyle=0\,,

(4.26)

\displaystyle{\cal N}^{(\zeta_{7})}_{\text{bubble}}(1,234)

\displaystyle=-\frac{13}{8}s_{1,\ell}^{2}s_{12}s_{13}s_{14}+4s_{1,\ell}(s_{12}^{4}+s_{13}^{4}+s_{14}^{4})-14s_{12}s_{13}s_{14}(s_{12}^{2}+s_{13}^{2}+s_{14}^{2})

\displaystyle\hskip-56.9055pt-\Big[s_{2,\ell}^{2}(6s_{12}s_{13}s_{14}{+}5s_{12}^{3})-8s_{2,\ell}s_{3,\ell}s_{14}^{3}+3s_{2,\ell}s_{12}^{2}(2s_{12}^{2}{-}3s_{13}^{2}{-}3s_{14}^{2})+\text{cyc}(2,3,4)\Big]\,,

and it is tedious but straightforward to obtain the next orders in

{\alpha^{\prime}}
along the same lines.

4.3
The five-point kinematic half integrand

The five-point worldsheet correlator in one-loop superstring amplitudes has been studied from
a variety of perspectives
Tsuchiya:1988va
;
Stieberger:2002wk
;
Mafra:2009wi
;
Mafra:2012kh
;
Mafra:2018qqe
,
and we shall now review the representation of
He:2017spx
for its analogue in the ambitwistor setup
Adamo:2013tsa
;
Geyer:2015bja
. Its kinematic dependence can be compactly encoded in the two-particle polarization
\epsilon_{12}^{\mu}
and field strength
f^{\mu\nu}_{12}

Mafra:2014oia
;
Mafra:2015vca
given by

\displaystyle\epsilon_{12}^{\mu}

\displaystyle=(k_{2}\cdot\epsilon_{1})\epsilon_{2}^{\mu}-(k_{1}\cdot\epsilon_{2})\epsilon_{1}^{\mu}+\frac{1}{2}(\epsilon_{1}\cdot\epsilon_{2})(k_{1}^{\mu}-k_{2}^{\mu})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\big[(k_{2}\cdot\epsilon_{1})\epsilon_{2}^{\mu}+(\epsilon_{1})_{\nu}f_{2}^{\nu\mu}-(1\leftrightarrow 2)\big]\,,

(4.27)

\displaystyle f_{12}^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=(k_{2}\cdot\epsilon_{1})f_{2}^{\mu\nu}-(k_{1}\cdot\epsilon_{2})f_{1}^{\mu\nu}+f_{1}^{\mu}{}_{\lambda}f_{2}^{\lambda\nu}-f_{2}^{\mu}{}_{\lambda}f_{1}^{\lambda\nu}

\displaystyle=k_{12}^{\mu}\epsilon_{12}^{\nu}-k_{12}^{\nu}\epsilon_{12}^{\mu}-(k_{1}\cdot k_{2})(\epsilon_{1}^{\mu}\epsilon_{2}^{\nu}-\epsilon_{1}^{\nu}\epsilon_{2}^{\mu})\,,

and subject to

\epsilon_{12}^{\mu}=-\epsilon_{21}^{\mu}\,,\ \ \ \ \ \ f_{12}^{\mu\nu}=-f_{21}^{\mu\nu}=-f_{12}^{\nu\mu}\,.

(4.28)

As a natural generalization of the four-point kinematic factor in (
4.2
),
the
t_{8}
-tensor (
4.1
) in the five-point correlator either contracts the
two-particle field strength or is dressed by additional polarization vectors
12
12

12

We are grateful to Carlos Mafra for discussions that led us to correct the
prefactor of the parity-odd terms to
\frac{1}{16}
in place of

\frac{i}{2}
as in earlier versions. The factor of
i
has been removed to account
for the minus sign in
\varepsilon_{10}^{\mu}(v_{1},v_{2},\ldots,v_{9})\eta_{\mu\nu}\varepsilon_{10}^{\nu}(w_{1},w_{2},\ldots,w_{9})=-\det(v_{i}\cdot w_{j})_{i,j=1,2,\ldots,9}
from the Minkowskian signature.

\displaystyle t_{8}(12,3,4,5)

\displaystyle=t_{8}(f_{12},f_{3},f_{4},f_{5})=-t_{8}(21,3,4,5)

(4.29)

\displaystyle t_{\pm}^{\mu}(1,2,3,4,5)

\displaystyle=\big[\epsilon_{1}^{\mu}t_{8}(f_{2},f_{3},f_{4},f_{5})+(1\leftrightarrow 2,3,4,5)\big]\pm\frac{1}{16}\varepsilon^{\mu}_{10}(\epsilon_{1},f_{2},f_{3},f_{4},f_{5})\,,

where
t_{8}(12,3,4,5)
and
t_{\pm}^{\mu}(1,2,3,4,5)
are permutation-invariant in
\{3,4,5\}
and

\{1,2,3,4,5\}
, respectively.
13
13

13

Momentum conservation also implies the
symmetry
\varepsilon^{\mu}_{10}(\epsilon_{1},f_{2},f_{3},f_{4},f_{5})=\varepsilon^{\mu}_{10}(\epsilon_{2},f_{1},f_{3},f_{4},f_{5})
of
the parity-odd contributions.
The
t_{\pm}
notation for the vector accommodates two sign choices for the
parity-odd term
\varepsilon^{\mu}_{10}(\epsilon_{1},f_{2},f_{3},f_{4},f_{5})=(\varepsilon_{10})^{\mu}{}_{\nu\lambda_{2}\rho_{2}\lambda_{3}\rho_{3}\lambda_{4}\rho_{4}\lambda_{5}\rho_{5}}\epsilon^{\nu}_{1}f^{\lambda_{2}\rho_{2}}_{2}f^{\lambda_{3}\rho_{3}}_{3}f^{\lambda_{4}\rho_{4}}_{4}f^{\lambda_{5}\rho_{5}}_{5}
in order to later on unify expressions for
type-IIA and type-IIB theories.

With the above building blocks, the five-point kinematic half integrand
can be compactly written as

{\cal I}_{5}(\ell)=\frac{\ell_{\mu}t_{+}^{\mu}(1,2,3,4,5)+\big[G_{12}t_{8}({12},3,4,5)+(1,2|1,2,3,4,5)\big]}{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}\sigma_{3}\sigma_{4}\sigma_{5}}

(4.30)

with the following shorthand for the Green function on the nodal sphere

G_{ij}=\frac{\sigma_{i}{+}\sigma_{j}}{2(\sigma_{i}{-}\sigma_{j})}\,.

(4.31)

The notation
+(1,2|1,2,3,4,5)
in (
4.30
) instructs to add the nine permutations of the term

G_{12}t_{8}(f_{12},f_{3},f_{4},f_{5})
in the square bracket with
12
replaced by any other pair
ij
with

1\leq i<j\leq 5
. By arranging the
\sigma_{j}
dependence of (
4.30
)
and (
4.31
) into Parke-Taylor
factors
He:2017spx
, we arrive at the following
pentagon numerators in the expansion (
2.8
),

\displaystyle{\cal I}_{5}(\ell)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\rho\in S_{5}}N_{+|\rho(12345)|-}(\ell){\rm PT}(+,\rho(1,2,3,4,5),-)\,,

\displaystyle N_{+|12345|-}(\ell)

\displaystyle=\ell_{\mu}t_{+}^{\mu}(1,2,3,4,5)-\frac{1}{2}\Big\{t_{8}({12},3,4,5)+t_{8}({13},2,4,5)

(4.32)

\displaystyle\qquad+t_{8}({14},2,3,5)+t_{8}({15},2,3,4)+t_{8}({23},1,4,5)+t_{8}({24},1,3,5)

\displaystyle\qquad+t_{8}({25},1,3,4)+t_{8}({34},1,2,5)+t_{8}({35},1,2,4)+t_{8}({45},1,2,3)\Big\}\,.

In contrast to the permutation-invariant four-point numerators
N_{+|\rho(1234)|-}{=}t_{8}(f_{1},f_{2},f_{3},f_{4})
,
the five-point numerators depend on the ordering
\rho
of the
pentagon. Its antisymmetric part

N_{+|12345|-}-N_{+|21345|-}=-t_{8}(12,3,4,5)

(4.33)

no longer depends on
\ell
, inherits the symmetry in
3,4,5
of the

t_{8}
-tensor
14
14

14

In other words, we also obtain
-t_{8}(12,3,4,5)

from
N_{+|31245|-}-N_{+|32145|-}
,

N_{+|34125|-}-N_{+|34215|-}
and
N_{+|34512|-}-N_{+|34521|-}

as well as their permutations in
3,4,5
.

and corresponds to a one-mass box numerator. The
structure of (
4.32
) and its compatibility with the
color-kinematics duality was firstly discussed in

Mafra:2014gja
. The ancestors of the kinematic building blocks
(
4.29
) in pure-spinor superspace described in the reference
can be readily imported to supersymmetrize the
t_{8}
-tensors in the

\alpha^{\prime}
-expansions below. Color-kinematics dual five-point
numerators in terms of four-dimensional spinor-helicity variables can
be found in
Carrasco:2011mn
;
He:2015wgf
.

For later reference, we note the linearized gauge variation
of the above building blocks which we shall implement via

\delta=\sum_{j=1}^{5}\omega_{j}k_{j}^{\mu}\frac{\partial}{\partial\epsilon_{j}^{\mu}}\,.

(4.34)

The formal bookkeeping variables
\omega_{j}
track the transformation

\epsilon_{j}\rightarrow k_{j}
in the
j^{\rm th}
leg,

\displaystyle\delta t_{8}(12,3,4,5)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}s_{12}\big[\omega_{1}t_{8}(f_{2},f_{3},f_{4},f_{5})-\omega_{2}t_{8}(f_{1},f_{3},f_{4},f_{5})\big]

\displaystyle\delta t_{+}^{\mu}(1,2,3,4,5)

\displaystyle=k_{1}^{\mu}\omega_{1}t_{8}(f_{2},f_{3},f_{4},f_{5})+(1\leftrightarrow 2,3,4,5)

\displaystyle\delta N_{+|12345|-}

\displaystyle=\frac{\omega_{1}}{2}\big(\ell_{1}^{2}-\ell^{2}\big)t_{8}(f_{2},f_{3},f_{4},f_{5})+\frac{\omega_{2}}{2}\big(\ell_{12}^{2}-\ell_{1}^{2}\big)t_{8}(f_{1},f_{3},f_{4},f_{5})

(4.35)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\omega_{3}}{2}\big(\ell_{123}^{2}-\ell_{12}^{2}\big)t_{8}(f_{1},f_{2},f_{4},f_{5})+\frac{\omega_{4}}{2}\big(\ell_{1234}^{2}-\ell_{123}^{2}\big)t_{8}(f_{1},f_{2},f_{3},f_{5})

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\omega_{5}}{2}\big(\ell^{2}-\ell_{1234}^{2}\big)t_{8}(f_{1},f_{2},f_{3},f_{4})\,,

where we introduced the shorthand notations

\ell_{1}=\ell+k_{1}\,,\qquad\ell_{12}=\ell+k_{12}\,,\qquad\ell_{123}=\ell+k_{123}\,,\qquad\ell_{1234}=\ell+k_{1234}\,,

(4.36)

and we have used five-point momentum conservation to simplify the variation of
N_{+|12345|-}
.
The operator (
4.34
) implementing linearized gauge variations is
engineered to mimic the BRST transformations of the supersymmetric
numerators in
Mafra:2014gja
.

4.4
Five points, open string

In contrast to the four-point case, already the field-theory limit of the one-loop
matrix elements (
3.1
) at five points is a combination of a pentagon
and five massive boxes,

\displaystyle A^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{\rm SYM}(1,2,3,4,5)

\displaystyle=\int\mathrm{d}^{D}\ell\,\bigg[\frac{N_{+|12345|-}}{\ell^{2}\ell_{1}^{2}\ell_{12}^{2}\ell_{123}^{2}\ell_{1234}^{2}}-\frac{t_{8}(12,3,4,5)}{s_{12}\ell^{2}\ell_{12}^{2}\ell_{123}^{2}\ell_{1234}^{2}}-\frac{t_{8}(23,1,4,5)}{s_{23}\ell^{2}\ell_{1}^{2}\ell_{123}^{2}\ell_{1234}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad-\frac{t_{8}(34,1,2,5)}{s_{34}\ell^{2}\ell_{1}^{2}\ell_{12}^{2}\ell_{1234}^{2}}-\frac{t_{8}(45,1,2,3)}{s_{12}\ell^{2}\ell_{1}^{2}\ell_{12}^{2}\ell_{123}^{2}}-\frac{t_{8}(51,2,3,4)}{s_{12}\ell_{1}^{2}\ell_{12}^{2}\ell_{123}^{2}\ell_{1234}^{2}}\bigg]\,.

(4.37)

In (
4.37
) and its
{\alpha^{\prime}}
-corrections, the recombination of the linearized
propagators to quadratic ones relies on shifts of loop momenta in
\ell_{\mu}t_{+}^{\mu}(1,2,3,4,5)

and the relation

(k_{1})_{\mu}t_{\pm}^{\mu}(1,2,3,4,5)=-t_{8}(12,3,4,5)-t_{8}(13,2,4,5)-t_{8}(14,2,3,5)-t_{8}(15,2,3,4)\,.

(4.38)

Based on the
{\alpha^{\prime}}
-expansion of the seven-point disk
integrals
Mafra:2016mcc
in the one-loop matrix element (
3.1
), we organize the corrections
to
eq.

4.37
via

\displaystyle A^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{\rm eff}(1,2,3,4,5)

\displaystyle=A^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{\rm SYM}(1,2,3,4,5)+\int\mathrm{d}^{D}\ell\,\bigg[\frac{N_{\text{box}}(1,2,3,45)}{\ell^{2}\ell_{1}^{2}\ell_{12}^{2}\ell_{123}^{2}}+\frac{N_{\text{triangle}}(1,2,345)}{\ell^{2}\ell_{1}^{2}\ell_{12}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{N_{\text{triangle}}(1,2,[34]5)}{\ell^{2}\ell_{1}^{2}\ell_{12}^{2}s_{34}}+\frac{N_{\text{triangle}}(1,2,3[45])}{\ell^{2}\ell_{1}^{2}\ell_{12}^{2}s_{45}}+\frac{N_{\text{triangle}}(1,23,45)}{\ell^{2}\ell_{1}^{2}\ell_{12}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{N_{\text{triangle}}(1,[23],45)}{\ell^{2}\ell_{1}^{2}\ell_{12}^{2}s_{23}}+\frac{N_{\text{triangle}}(1,23,[45])}{\ell^{2}\ell_{1}^{2}\ell_{12}^{2}s_{45}}+\frac{N_{\text{bubble}}(12,345)}{\ell^{2}\ell_{12}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{N_{\text{bubble}}([12],345)}{\ell^{2}\ell_{12}^{2}s_{12}}+\frac{N_{\text{bubble}}(12,[34]5)}{\ell^{2}\ell_{12}^{2}s_{34}}+\frac{N_{\text{bubble}}(12,3[45])}{\ell^{2}\ell_{12}^{2}s_{45}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{N_{\text{bubble}}(1,2345)}{\ell^{2}\ell_{1}^{2}}+\frac{N_{\text{bubble}}(1,[23]45)}{\ell^{2}\ell_{1}^{2}s_{23}}+\frac{N_{\text{bubble}}(1,2[34]5)}{\ell^{2}\ell_{1}^{2}s_{34}}

(4.39)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{N_{\text{bubble}}(1,23[45])}{\ell^{2}\ell_{1}^{2}s_{45}}+\frac{1}{5}\frac{N_{\text{tadpole}}(12345)}{\ell^{2}}+\text{cyc}_{\ell}(1,2,3,4,5)\bigg]+\mathcal{O}({\alpha^{\prime}}^{6})\,,

where square brackets as in
N_{\text{triangle}}(1,[23],45)
or
N_{\text{bubble}}([12],345)
indicate the
presence of propagators
s^{-1}_{ij}
as opposed to the contact diagrams
N_{\text{triangle}}(1,23,45)
and

N_{\text{bubble}}(12,345)
at different mass dimensions. The notation
\text{cyc}_{\ell}(1,2,3,4,5)
is
again understood to include a shift of loop momentum such that
\ell
points from leg 5 to leg 1
which may affect the numerators as exemplified in (
4.8
). We give complete results up to
{\alpha^{\prime}}^{5}
order,

\displaystyle N_{\Gamma}

\displaystyle={\alpha^{\prime}}^{2}\zeta_{2}N_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{2})}+{\alpha^{\prime}}^{3}\zeta_{3}N_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{3})}+{\alpha^{\prime}}^{4}\zeta_{2}^{2}N_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{2}^{2})}+{\alpha^{\prime}}^{5}\Big[\zeta_{5}N_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{5})}+\zeta_{2}\zeta_{3}N_{\Gamma}^{(\zeta_{2}\zeta_{3})}\Big]\,.

(4.40)

The

): the coefficients of

\displaystyle N^{(\zeta_{2})}_{\text{box}}(1,2,3,45)

4.5
Five points, closed string

For the one-loop matrix elements (
3.2
) of closed strings, the
ordering-dependence of the five-point numerators in (
4.32
) does not
allow for a straightforward uplift of the four-point relation (
4.19
) to
single-valued open-string matrix elements (though
n
-point relations of this type
are available in (
3.2
) at the level of linearized propagators).
Instead, we insert the five-point numerators into (
3.2
) and
assemble the
{\alpha^{\prime}}
-expansion of the sphere integrals from
single-valued seven-point disk integrals. At the leading orders in

\alpha^{\prime}
, we have

\displaystyle M^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{5,{\rm eff}}

\displaystyle=M^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{5,{\rm SUGRA}}+2\int\mathrm{d}^{D}\ell\,\bigg[\frac{1}{4}\frac{{\cal N}_{\text{box}}(1,2,3,45)}{\ell^{2}(\ell{+}k_{1})^{2}(\ell{+}k_{12})^{2}(\ell{+}k_{123})^{2}}+\frac{1}{12}\frac{{\cal N}_{\text{triangle}}(1,2,345)}{\ell^{2}(\ell{+}k_{1})^{2}(\ell{+}k_{12})^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\!+\frac{1}{4}\frac{{\cal N}_{\text{triangle}}(1,2,[34]5)}{\ell^{2}(\ell{+}k_{1})^{2}(\ell{+}k_{12})^{2}s_{34}}+\frac{1}{4}\frac{{\cal N}_{\text{triangle}}(1,23,[45])}{\ell^{2}(\ell{+}k_{1})^{2}(\ell{+}k_{123})^{2}s_{45}}+\frac{1}{48}\frac{\mathcal{N}_{\text{bubble}}(1,2345)}{\ell^{2}(\ell{+}k_{1})^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\!+\frac{1}{8}\frac{\mathcal{N}_{\text{bubble}}(1,[23]45)}{\ell^{2}(\ell{+}k_{1})^{2}s_{23}}+\frac{1}{24}\frac{\mathcal{N}_{\text{bubble}}([12],345)}{\ell^{2}(\ell{+}k_{1})^{2}s_{12}}+\text{perm}(1{,}2{,}3{,}4{,}5)\bigg]+{\cal O}({\alpha^{\prime}}^{6})\,.

(4.46)

In this work, we have computed the numerators up to
{\alpha^{\prime}}^{5}\zeta_{5}
order,

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{\Gamma}

\displaystyle={\alpha^{\prime}}^{3}\zeta_{3}\mathcal{N}^{(\zeta_{3})}+{\alpha^{\prime}}^{5}\zeta_{5}\mathcal{N}^{(\zeta_{5})}\,.

(4.47)

M^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{5,{\rm SUGRA}}
of supergravity

4.5.1
Type IIA versus type IIB

Note that
M^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{5,{\rm SUGRA}}
and (
4.48a
) feature
bilinears in the vector building block
t_{\pm}^{\mu}
in (
4.29
) that
differ between the type-IIA and IIB theories: the relative sign of the parity-odd
terms need to be chosen as
t_{+}^{\mu}\bar{t}_{+}^{\nu}
for the chiral type-IIB theory
and as
t_{+}^{\mu}\bar{t}_{-}^{\nu}
in the non-chiral type-IIA theory. Accordingly, the
one-loop matrix elements in the two theories differ by

\displaystyle M^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{5,{\rm eff}}\,\Big|_{\rm IIA}

\displaystyle=M^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{5,{\rm eff}}\,\Big|_{\rm IIB}-\frac{1}{8}\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{D}\ell}{\ell^{2}}\,\ell_{\mu}\varepsilon_{10}^{\mu}(\bar{\epsilon}_{1},\bar{f}_{2},\bar{f}_{3},\bar{f}_{4},\bar{f}_{5})

(4.49)

\displaystyle\quad\times\lim_{k_{\pm}\rightarrow\pm\ell}\sum_{\gamma,\rho\in S_{5}}J\big(+,\gamma(1,2,3,4,5),-|+,\rho(1,2,3,4,5),-\big)N_{+|\rho(12345)|-}(\ell)\,,

see (
4.32
) for the numerators
N_{+|\rho(12345)|-}(\ell)
.
When the external states are chosen to be five gravitons
h^{5}

with
\bar{\epsilon}_{j}\rightarrow\epsilon_{j}
(rather than
B
-fields
or dilatons), the general formula (
4.49
) for the difference specializes as follows

\displaystyle M^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{5,{\rm eff}}\,\Big|^{h^{5}}_{\rm IIA}=M^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{5,{\rm eff}}\,\Big|^{h^{5}}_{\rm IIB}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{128}\int\frac{\mathrm{d}^{D}\ell}{\ell^{2}}\,\ell_{\mu}\varepsilon_{10}^{\mu}(\epsilon_{1},f_{2},f_{3},f_{4},f_{5})\ell_{\nu}\varepsilon_{10}^{\nu}(\epsilon_{1},f_{2},f_{3},f_{4},f_{5})

(4.50)

\displaystyle\quad\times\lim_{k_{\pm}\rightarrow\pm\ell}\sum_{\gamma,\rho\in S_{5}}J(+,\gamma(1,2,3,4,5),-|+,\rho(1,2,3,4,5),-)\,,

where only the tensor structure
\ell_{\mu}\ell_{\nu}\sim\eta_{\mu\nu}
contributes to the loop
integral.

4.5.2
Higher orders in
\alpha^{\prime}

At the orders of
\alpha^{\prime 5}\zeta_{5}(D^{4}R^{4}+D^{2}R^{5}+R^{6})

and beyond, expressions similar to (
4.48
) can be obtained from (
3.2
).
However, the computational effort grows drastically with the number of terms in the
\alpha^{\prime}
-expansion
of sphere integrals which need to be combined to quadratic propagators as in (
4.46
).
As will be detailed below, the reorganization (
5.34
) of the five-point one-loop matrix element
in terms of certain polarization-independent integrals “
\langle E|E\rangle
” with
quadratic-propagator representations furnishes an
improved starting point to obtain explicit results at higher orders in
{\alpha^{\prime}}
.
By analogy with the four-point results in (
4.24
) and (
4.25
), the onset of
external and two-mass bubble diagrams is expected at the orders of
{\alpha^{\prime}}^{5}\zeta_{5}

and
{\alpha^{\prime}}^{6}\zeta_{3}^{2}
, respectively.

5
One-loop matrix elements and quadratic propagators from superstrings

In this section, we relate the one-loop matrix elements to superstring amplitudes and reorganize
the transition from linearized to quadratic propagators according to the homology invariance
of worldsheet correlators on the torus.

5.1
Motivation from chiral splitting

In earlier sections, the proposal (
3.1
) and (
3.2
) for one-loop matrix elements of higher-mass-dimension
operators has been motivated from the ambitwistor-string perspective. We shall now provide a complementary
motivation from certain limits of one-loop amplitudes of conventional strings based on the chiral-splitting
formalism
DHoker:1988pdl
;
DHoker:1989cxq
.

The key idea of chiral splitting is to identify loop momenta as
the joint zero modes of the worldsheet fields
\partial_{z}X^{\mu}
and
\partial_{\bar{z}}X^{\mu}
in
the left- and right-moving sector. By separating the loop-momentum integral
from the rest of the path integral that defines the string integrand, the contributions of the left- and
right movers can be kept in a factorized form.
In particular, the evaluation and simplification of the correlation function of vertex operators that
determines the basis of kinematic factors can be performed separately for the two chiral halves
of the string amplitude. Accordingly, chiral splitting is the driving force in the recent construction
of the correlators in multiparticle one-loop
Mafra:2018qqe
, five-point
two-loop
DHoker:2020prr
and three-loop four-point
Geyer:2021oox
string amplitudes.

In the chiral-splitting approach to one-loop closed-string amplitudes, the integrand of
16
16

16

For ease of notation, the chiral-splitting representation (
5.1
) is adapted to the case of
real invariants
\ell^{2}
,
\ell\cdot k_{i}
and
k_{i}\cdot k_{j}
. One can still accommodate complex kinematics
by employing
-k_{j}^{\ast}
,
-\ell^{\ast}
in the place of
k_{j}
,
\ell
in one of the chiral halves
before complex conjugation of
{\cal K}_{n}(\ell,\tau)\,{\cal J}_{n}(\ell,\tau)
. In this way, the real parts
of
\tau
and
z_{j}
drop out from the first and second term in the exponent of (
5.2
) when
combining the left- and right-movers.

M^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{n,{\rm closed}}=\int\mathrm{d}^{D}\ell\int_{\mathfrak{F}}\mathrm{d}^{2}\tau\int_{\mathfrak{T}_{\tau}^{n-1}}\mathrm{d}^{2}z_{2}\ldots\mathrm{d}^{2}z_{n}\,\big|{\cal K}_{n}(\ell,\tau)\,{\cal J}_{n}(\ell,\tau)\big|^{2}

(5.1)

is the absolute-value square of the chiral amplitude
{\cal K}_{n}{\cal J}_{n}
which is a
meromorphic function of the moduli
z_{j}
and
\tau
with
z_{1}=0
by translation invariance.
We have organized the chiral amplitude into the Koba-Nielsen factor

{\cal J}_{n}(\ell,\tau)=\exp\bigg(2\pi i\alpha^{\prime}\tau\ell^{2}+4\pi i\alpha^{\prime}\sum_{j=1}^{n}(\ell\cdot k_{j})z_{j}+2\alpha^{\prime}\sum_{1\leq i<j}^{n}k_{i}\cdot k_{j}\log\theta_{1}(z_{ij},\tau)\bigg)

(5.2)

and a kinematic factor
{\cal K}_{n}
that carries all the dependence on the external polarization.
While (
5.2
) is the correlator of the universal plane-wave parts
e^{ik_{j}\cdot X}
of vertex operators,
the Wick contractions of the remaining worldsheet fields determine
{\cal K}_{n}
in terms of derivatives
of the odd Jacobi theta function
17
17

17

More precisely, the chiral correlators
{\cal K}_{n}
for massless
states in type-II, heterotic and bosonic string theories are
polynomials in loop momenta, holomorphic Eisenstein series
and expansion coefficients of a meromorphic Kronecker–Eisenstein series
Dolan:2007eh
;
Broedel:2014vla
;
Gerken:2018jrq
.

\theta_{1}(z,\tau)=2iq^{1/8}\sin(\pi z)\prod_{j=1}^{\infty}(1-q^{j})(1-e^{2\pi iz}q^{j})(1-e^{-2\pi iz}q^{j})=-\theta_{1}(-z,\tau)\,,\ \ \ \ q=e^{2\pi i\tau}\,.

(5.3)

In fact, the maximally supersymmetric
{\cal K}_{n}
are degree-
(n{-}4)
polynomials in loop momenta,
see (
5.9
) below for a relation to the half integrand
{\cal I}_{n}(\ell)
in (
2.7
),
(
4.2
) and (
4.32
).

Finally, the integration domains for the moduli in (
5.1
) are the fundamental domain
\mathfrak{F}
of the modular group
{\rm SL}_{2}(\mathbb{Z})
and the torus worldsheet
\mathfrak{T}_{\tau}=\frac{\mathbb{C}}{\mathbb{Z}+\tau\mathbb{Z}}
in its parametrization as a parallelogram.

The open-string counterpart of (
5.1
) in the chiral-splitting formalism is

A^{\textrm{1-loop}}_{{\rm open}}(\gamma(1,2,\ldots,n))=\int\mathrm{d}^{D}\ell\int_{0}^{i\infty}\mathrm{d}\tau\int_{\mathfrak{C}_{\tau}(\gamma)}\mathrm{d}z_{2}\ldots\mathrm{d}z_{n}\,{\cal K}_{n}(\ell,\tau)\,|{\cal J}_{n}(\ell,\tau)|\,,

(5.4)

where
z_{1}=0
by translation invariance, the modular parameter
\tau
is integrated over the positive
imaginary axis instead of
\mathfrak{F}
, and the punctures
z_{i}=\tau u_{i}
(with real
u_{i}
)
are inserted on the cylinder boundary

\mathfrak{C}_{\tau}(\gamma)=\bigoplus_{j=1}^{n}\{-\tfrac{1}{2}<u_{\gamma(j+1)}<u_{\gamma(j+2)}<\ldots<u_{\gamma(n)}<0<u_{\gamma(2)}<u_{\gamma(3)}<\ldots<u_{\gamma(j)}<\tfrac{1}{2}\}\,.

(5.5)

The integration domains in (
5.4
) are tailored to the amplitude contributions from planar
cylinder diagrams and can be adapted to the non-planar cylinder or the Möbius strip by shifting

z_{j}\rightarrow z_{j}+\frac{1}{2}
or
\tau\rightarrow\tau+\frac{1}{2}
, respectively
Polchinski:1998rq
.
As visualized in Figure
8a
, the cylinder worldsheet in (
5.4
)
and (
5.5
) arises from a rectangular torus whose homology cycle
\mathfrak{B}
sets the cylinder boundary where all the state
insertions occur. The second
cylinder boundary relevant for non-planar one-loop open-string amplitudes is displaced by half the
homology cycle
\mathfrak{A}
, i.e. by
z_{j}\rightarrow z_{j}+\frac{1}{2}
. As reflected by the appearance
of negative
\Im(z_{i})
in (
5.5
), we have chosen the fundamental cell of the parental torus to be
the rectangle with corners at
\pm\frac{\tau}{2}
and
1\pm\frac{\tau}{2}
, and the cylinder covers its half
bounded by
\pm\frac{\tau}{2}
and
\frac{1}{2}\pm\frac{\tau}{2}
seen in Figure
8a
.

Parametrization of the integration domain on the cylinder boundary in planar one-loop open-string amplitudes.

5.1.1
The
\tau\rightarrow i\infty
degeneration

One-loop amplitudes of SYM and supergravity arise in the limit
\tau\rightarrow i\infty

where the
\mathfrak{A}
-cycle pinches, and the torus degenerates to a nodal sphere.
In other words, the field-theory limit
{\alpha^{\prime}}\rightarrow 0

is traditionally performed while keeping the dimensionful quantity
{\alpha^{\prime}}\Im\tau
finite and identifying it with
one of the Schwinger parameters of Feynman integrals

Green:1982sw
;
Bern:1990cu
;
Bern:1990ux
;
Bern:1991aq
;
Bern:1991an
;
Bern:1993wt
;
Dunbar:1994bn

The
\tau\rightarrow i\infty
limits of the constituents of the one-loop open-string amplitude (

\sigma_{j}
on the nodal sphere

\sigma_{j}=e^{2\pi iz_{j}}\,,\ \ \ \ \ \ \mathrm{d}z_{j}=\frac{\mathrm{d}\sigma_{j}}{2\pi i\sigma_{j}}\,,\ \ \ \ \ \ \mathrm{d}^{2}z_{j}=\frac{\mathrm{d}^{2}\sigma_{j}}{4\pi^{2}\sigma_{j}\bar{\sigma}_{j}}\,.

(5.6)

First, the cylinder ordering in the parametrization of (

) tends to a cyclic combination of disk
orderings (
2.24
) in the
\sigma_{j}
-variable, see Figure

,

\lim_{\tau\rightarrow i\infty}\mathfrak{C}_{\tau}(1,2,\ldots,n)=\bigoplus_{j=1}^{n}\mathfrak{D}_{j}\,,\ \ \ \ \ \ \mathfrak{D}_{j}=\mathfrak{D}(+,j,j{-}1,\ldots,2,1,n,n{-}1,\ldots,j{+}1,-)\,,

(5.7)

where
\sigma_{+}=0
and
\sigma_{-}\rightarrow\infty
as in (

). Second, the chiral Koba-Nielsen factor
(

) turns into the forward limit of the tree-level one in (

) after removing the
scaling by
e^{2\pi i\alpha^{\prime}\tau\ell^{2}}
,

\lim_{\tau\rightarrow i\infty}e^{-2\pi i\alpha^{\prime}\tau\ell^{2}}{\cal J}_{n}(\ell,\tau)=\prod_{j=1}^{n}\sigma_{j+}^{2{\alpha^{\prime}}\ell\cdot k_{j}}\prod_{1\leq i<j}^{n}|\sigma_{ij}|^{2{\alpha^{\prime}}k_{i}\cdot k_{j}}\,,

(5.8)

where we used
\sigma_{1}=1
and momentum conservation
18
18

18

More precisely, we start
from the degeneration
\log\theta_{1}(z_{ij},\tau)=\frac{i\pi\tau}{4}+\log(\frac{\sigma_{ij}}{\sqrt{\sigma_{i}\sigma_{j}}})+{\cal O}(e^{2\pi i\tau})
and apply momentum conservation in the form of
\sum_{j\neq i}^{n}s_{ij}=0
to cancel the contributions from
\frac{i\pi\tau}{4}
and
-\frac{1}{2}(\log\sigma_{i}+\log\sigma_{j})
to

\sum_{1\leq i<j}^{n}s_{ij}\log\theta_{1}(z_{ij},\tau)=\sum_{1\leq i<j}^{n}s_{ij}\log\sigma_{ij}+{\cal O}(e^{2\pi i\tau})
.
.
Third, the polarization dependent part
{\cal K}_{n}
of the chiral correlator yields the kinematic half integrand
of the ambitwistor string once its
z_{ij}
-dependence has been brought into logarithmic form via integration by parts
He:2017spx
,

{\cal I}_{n}(\ell)=\lim_{\tau\rightarrow i\infty}\frac{{\cal K}_{n}(\ell,\tau)\,}{(2\pi i)^{n-4}\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}\ldots\sigma_{n}}\,.

(5.9)

The inverse factors of
\sigma_{j}
in (

) stem from the conversion of
\mathrm{d}z_{j}
into
\mathrm{d}\sigma_{j}
in (

). The four- and five-point instances of
{\cal I}_{n}(\ell)
in (

) and (

) arise from

\displaystyle{\cal K}_{4}(\ell,\tau)

\displaystyle=t_{8}(f_{1},f_{2},f_{3},f_{4})\,,

(5.10)

\displaystyle{\cal K}_{5}(\ell,\tau)

\displaystyle=2\pi i\ell_{\mu}t_{+}^{\mu}(1,2,3,4,5)+\big[\partial_{z_{1}}\log\theta_{1}(z_{12},\tau)t_{8}({12},3,4,5)+(1,2|1,2,3,4,5)\big]\,,

which can be checked by
\lim_{\tau\rightarrow i\infty}\partial_{z_{1}}\log\theta_{1}(z_{12},\tau)=2\pi iG_{12}
,
see (

) for the five-point kinematic factors and (

) for the nodal Green function

.
The
(n\leq 5)

) are inevitably in logarithmic form, while the analogous six-
and seven-point correlators

admit both logarithmic and non-logarithmic representations that
are related by integration-by-parts manipulations detailed in the reference. The vector building blocks in
the bilinear

of the closed-string five-point amplitude (

) are
